Then she opened her eyes and discovered that the noise came from Cassian attempting to make breakfast in a kitchen whose cupboard doors opened in unexpected directions.","\"You have been defeated by a hinge,\" she observed.","\"A temporary discrepancy between theory and practice.\"","He set down bread, cheese, and two eggs that had survived his attention. Beside them lay her admission card, retrieved from the pocket she had searched twice the previous night. Mara was worried about the demonstration, the missing page, and the possibility that gratitude had become her most embarrassing weakness. The eggs offered a useful subject on which to concentrate.","\"Did you read my notes?\" she asked.","\"No. I moved them away from the butter. That was the extent of my intellectual contribution.\"","At the academy, Holt checked the object against its recorded condition. The original lamp would remain disconnected. Its heat had contributed to the crack, so the demonstration would use a cool external light mounted without altering the old fittings. Mara had documented the choice. Preserving an object did not require repeating every condition that had damaged it.","The third sample support still held. She tested it once more before touching the screen. There were moments when an extra test was unnecessary and moments when being impatient could have an impact on everything that followed. She intended to learn the difference before other people's property paid for her education.","Cassian carried the theater to the auditorium. Ada followed with the condition sheets. An independent observer compared the seals, while a technician verified the isolated controller. The former chairperson of the academy had once described these precautions as a restraint on inspiration. Mara considered them a way to give inspiration somewhere safe to stand.","The audience was larger than before. A popular presenter sat in the front row, carrying a best-seller on the history of famous frauds. Mara wondered whether he had brought it as reading material or an accusation. Beside him, a woman pointed towards Cassian with the recognition of someone finding a high-fashion label inside an ordinary coat.","Mara kept her eyes on the worktable.","The teacher from the charter school came to stand beside her before the first bell. Her elderly father had arrived in his best coat, carrying a paper bag of biscuits he had been told were not permitted in the auditorium.","\"He thinks the judges will be hungry,\" she said.","Mara looked at the anxious man, then at the five people preparing to decide their futures. \"A sound theory.\"","The teacher slipped a biscuit into her hand. It was broken at one edge and tasted of ginger. Mara ate it behind the curtain, suddenly less interested in the audience's expensive clothes. There were people in the room who understood exactly how much a chance could cost.","Lucien went first. His silver clock ran evenly, and his explanation was clear. He acknowledged that its replacement spring had a different tension from the original and described how that affected the motion. The judges asked questions about accuracy and retention of old material. He answered without pretending the object had become new.","When he came down from the stage, Mara said, \"That was good.\"","\"I know,\" he replied. Then he glanced at her hands. \"The third sample held?\"","\"So far.\"","\"Then stop testing the air with your fingers. You'll wear them out before you begin.\"","It was blunt, useful advice. She folded her hands and waited for her number.","The host introduced her as an independent applicant. He did not mention her engagement or her report. Mara was grateful enough to forgive him the cake. She explained what the intervention would demonstrate: a mechanism freed from an obstruction, an original screen supported against further movement, and a modern light used only for safe viewing. She could show function under those conditions. She could not promise that the artifact would run indefinitely.","\"Why keep the damaged screen?\" a judge asked. \"A transparent substitute would make the image smoother.\"","\"It would,\" Mara said. \"It would also remove evidence of how the old image was made. I am not trying to make the object younger. I am trying to keep its history readable.\"","She placed the modern replacement beside a photograph of the original surface. The difference was visible without specialised equipment. The old screen scattered the light unevenly, creating the soft border the designer had intended. Its flaw was part of its present condition. Its texture was part of the work.","The control was set to twelve. The independent observer read it aloud. Mara asked Cassian to hold the recording card in view and then step away. It was their smallest public negotiation yet, but he gave her the space as carefully as he had steadied the broken stair.","She connected the power.","The first ticket moved through its slot. The monkey bowed. The woman in blue crossed the stage, her basket swinging. On the screen behind them, the little figures became taller than the audience watching them. Mara heard a child in the back row whisper that the monkey looked important.","Then the light caught the repaired line.","It ran across the woman's shadow like a fine thread. Mara felt the old urge to conceal it, to make the performance perfect for the people ready to think her unworthy. She could have moved the lamp a fraction. The line would disappear from the camera, although not from the object.","She left it where it was.","\"That is the crack,\" she said. \"You can see the support below it. It is removable, and the next conservator will know where I worked.\"","The second ticket caught halfway through the mechanism.","Her heart gave one hard beat. She stopped the motor before the paper bent. The silence in the room seemed to acquire height and weight.","\"A repeated failure?\" the presenter in the front row asked.","Mara looked at the ticket through the inspection window. Its edge was curled. The recorded condition sheet showed the same curl before the demonstration. She had not flattened the old paper because doing so would require a separate assessment. The mechanism had worked with the first ticket, which was straighter. She had expected the second might catch. Expecting a problem had not made encountering it pleasant.","\"A limitation I recorded,\" she said. \"The motor is stopped. I can withdraw this ticket without pulling it against the rollers, then use the sample card to demonstrate the remaining motion. Would the panel prefer that, or the test to end here?\"","Hart asked the independent observer to confirm the condition note. He did. The panel permitted the sample card, provided the distinction remained explicit. Mara placed the old ticket in a transparent container before proceeding. Nothing was hidden under the table or smoothed into a claim of complete success.","The mechanism finished its round. A little door opened at the back of the painted stage. Mara had repaired its hinge the previous evening, using a reversible support rather than a stronger pin that would have split the surrounding wood. The door revealed a paper sky, faded from blue to the shade of an early morning cloud.","For a moment she saw what the maker had wanted: not an expensive gadget, but a small surprise offered to a roomful of people. Some of those people would have been children. Some would have been tired adults who had paid for one evening free of their own concerns. The toy did not need a miracle. It needed someone willing to care about its ordinary purpose.","She disconnected the power.","The applause began at the back. It spread unevenly, with a pause before the front rows joined. Mara did not know whether it meant approval of her work or relief that nothing had exploded. She accepted it in either case.","The judges asked about the alloy in the frame, the probability of further wear, and the budget required for ongoing care. She admitted that one question about the old coating lay outside her expertise. A specialised analysis would be needed before she could answer it. The admission cost her less than she expected.","\"And what would you have done differently?\" Hart asked.","\"Checked the shared control's history before accepting it. Documented my submission after upload, as well as before. And eaten more than half an egg this morning.\"","The last answer escaped before she could retrieve it. Holt laughed. Cassian looked so quietly pleased that Mara forgot, for a dangerous second, everyone else in the room.","Offstage, he handed her a towel. There was a dark mark on her wrist from the frame. She rubbed at it, then stopped when it did not come away.","\"Later,\" he said.","\"It will look as though I worked.\"","\"A formidable accusation.\"","She smiled. The formal finding would come tomorrow. There was still a disputed document to resolve, a grant to award, and an unknown person who wanted her to withdraw. None of that had disappeared.","But the theater had moved, and the line had remained visible."],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"Why does Mara leave the repaired line visible during the final Its carved back rose above every other chair like a threat. Beside it, a little card read LORD VEY, SPECIAL PATRON. His actual place, among the assistants, had disappeared.","She carried the gold chair off the stage herself. The publicity director hurried after her, protesting that it was extremely valuable.","\"Then we ought to keep it out of danger,\" Mara said. \"He has already broken one rehearsal chair this week.\"","By the time Cassian arrived, there was an ordinary seat beside Ada's. Mara had placed his number on it. He examined the arrangement, looked towards the abandoned throne, and began to smile.","\"My standing appears to have declined.\"","\"Your posture may improve.\"","He bent closer. \"Have I offended the committee?\"","\"Not yet. There is still an hour.\"","The ceremony was meant to announce the fellowship result. First, Dr. Hart had a statement to make. The independent document examiner had completed the narrow review requested by the panel. Mara's original upload had been recovered from the retained submission record. It matched Holt's early printed copy. The disputed paragraph was absent.","The paragraph had entered later through the catalogue-reference service. An imported reference package had supplied the text, and the export process had attached it to the report without distinguishing it from the candidate's own writing. The same process had generated the misleading confirmation label. In a controlled test, the examiner reproduced those steps from the retained input.","Hart delivered the explanation in less than two minutes. She did not name a conspirator or suggest that a technical reconstruction explained every suspicious event. An investigation into the package's origin would continue. The academic allegation against Mara, however, was withdrawn.","The room shifted around the words. Mara had thought she was prepared for relief. She had not expected the rush of anger that came with it: all the hours she had spent defending a sentence, all the faces she had watched decide that they already knew her.","A man from the donor committee raised his hand. \"One assumes Miss Vale will accept that the academy was merely exercising appropriate caution.\"","Mara stood before Hart could answer. \"Appropriate caution would have said the authorship was disputed. The public notice said I had confirmed it. I'd like the correction to be equally easy to find.\"","The man looked towards the cameras. June Bell, sitting behind one, lifted her hand in a cheerful wave.","Hart turned to the clerk. \"Publish the correction through the same channels as the allegation. Attach it to every distributed version.\"","Mara sat. Ada pressed a paper cup into her hand. It contained water, which was sensible, although champagne would have matched her feelings better.","\"A devastatingly moderate demand,\" Ada whispered.","\"I'm trying to broaden my repertoire.\"","Then came the scores. Lucien received the prize for mechanical analysis. The former schoolteacher won a teaching residency. The school leaver was offered a paid apprenticeship, which made him look briefly as though he had forgotten how to breathe. Mara clapped until her palms hurt. She knew what it meant to enter a place expecting only the chance to be turned away.","The fellowship went last. The chairman began with a speech about merit. Mara's patience receded towards the exit. He praised research, innovation, self-reliance, and the importance of serving people whose objects carried no famous signature. She wondered whether he was going to thank every previous donor before naming an actual person.","\"The board's decision,\" he said, \"was unanimous.\"","Cassian had gone very still beside his ordinary chair.","\"The Open Fellowship is awarded to Mara Vale.\"","For a second she stayed where she was. Ada made an impatient noise and pushed the water out of her hand. Then Mara was walking down the aisle, hearing her own name repeated somewhere behind her, with no invented family attached to it.","The medal was smaller than she expected. The grant letter was heavier. It offered six months of paid work, supervised access to the collection's research records, and the right to propose a public conservation project. The conditions were clear. She read enough to find the payment date before anyone could confuse her expression with modesty.","The chairman handed her the medal. \"Your persistence has been most impressive.\"","\"I'm glad it became an advantage eventually.\"","He laughed because the cameras were watching. Holt laughed because she understood.","Mara turned to the audience. For years she had imagined that a victory would make her taller. Instead, the room seemed to have become less enormous. She could see individual faces: the volunteer from registration, the teacher, Lucien, Ada wiping one eye and pretending to inspect the ceiling. Cassian was not clapping wildly. He was watching her with such unguarded pride that she nearly lost the sentence she had prepared.","\"My mother taught me to leave a repair readable,\" she said. \"If someone comes after us, they should be able to see where we helped and where we made mistakes. I'd like to use this fellowship to work on objects whose owners don't usually get invited into rooms like this.\"","It was not a grand speech. It was hers. The applause came before she could decide whether it was enough.","The publicity director tried to guide Cassian onto the stage for photographs. Mara saw him glance at her, then gesture towards the other assistants instead. A minute later, every entrant stood beside the person who had helped carry the work. The photographer objected that the group was too wide.","\"Use a wider lens,\" June suggested.","The photographer did. Cassian found himself at the end of the line, holding the school leaver's tool case while the young man fastened his apprentice badge. Mara would have paid money for that photograph if the fellowship had not already made money a dangerously exciting subject.","Afterward, Lucien offered his hand. \"Congratulations.\"","\"You sound almost cheerful.\"","\"I have decided that losing to a careful person is less irritating than winning against a careless one. I may revise the opinion tomorrow.\"","She shook his hand. \"I'll preserve the current version.\"","At the reception, a former hiring administrator approached with an invitation to discuss employment. He was the man who had once told her that practical experience was difficult to assess without a degree. Mara recognized him immediately. Recognition arrived a moment later on his face.","\"Our requirements have developed,\" he said.","\"So has my fee.\"","Cassian turned aside with a cough. Ada did not bother hiding her laughter.","Mara accepted the administrator's card rather than punishing him further. She wanted work, fair rates, and a chance to stop bargaining over every hour of her life. She did not need to surrender any of those things to prove that she could be gracious.","Outside, June caught her before the crowd could. \"One interview. No surprise questions about your engagement.\"","\"I thought you lived for surprise questions.\"","\"I'm developing other interests. For example, the correction nobody wanted to print.\"","They recorded a short statement beside the garden. June asked what the fellowship would let Mara do next. Mara spoke about the public collection and said nothing about a hidden page or a missing witness. When a second reporter called for a photograph with her fiancé, June stepped into his line of sight and began asking him whose outlet he represented.","Mara escaped through the side gate with Cassian and Ada. The sensation was glorious. She had spent the week trying to enter the academy. Now it required three people to help her leave.","At the corner, Cassian took a small paper parcel from his pocket.","\"If that is another contract, I may scream.\"","\"Open it.\"","Inside lay a narrow leather case for her tools. No crest, no jewels, no lock that required his permission. Her old roll had split at the seam two days earlier. He had noticed.","\"I can afford my own now,\" she said, touching the stitching.","\"I know.\"","That was the answer that undid her. She could buy it herself. He had chosen it because he wanted her to have something good, not because he wanted to be necessary.","She slipped the medal into its smallest pocket. \"You'll have to carry the theater tomorrow. This doesn't cancel your duties.\"","\"I had hoped it might secure my position.\"","Her phone vibrated before she could think of an answer. The first grant payment had arrived. Mara opened the message, closed it, and opened it again.","Cassian watched her face. \"Good news?\"","\"The numbers are the same twice. I thought they might change if I looked too quickly.\"","At the bakery, she bought the large cake she had spent a year pretending to dislike. The baker, who knew the history of that particular dislike, added four forks without a word. Mara insisted on paying for those too. They ate standing beneath the awning, the medal safe among her tools, while Cassian submitted to being given the slice with the most icing. For once, nothing important was waiting to be stolen.","Ada looked between them. \"If you two finish negotiating the labor market, there is a bakery across the road.
